> AGENT

Done. I filed issue #3 with 13 new detector proposals, each with:
- **What was found** in the real codebase
- **How to detect it programmatically** (AST analysis, regex, cross-file comparison)
- **Suggested severity tier**

The key ones:

| Detector | What it catches | How |
|---|---|---|
| `monster_function` | Functions >150-300 LOC (your example) | AST: FunctionDef line spans |
| `duplicated_constants` | Same constant defined in 8+ files | AST: match name+value pairs across files |
| `mixed_logging` | print() + logger + dprint in same file | Grep for multiple logging patterns |
| `copy_paste_block` | Near-identical blocks within a file (like the 3x thumbnail extraction) | AST sliding window hash comparison |
| `magic_string_enum` | Task type strings as bare literals in 5+ files | String literal cross-referencing |
| `dead_function` | No-op functions (just `return`) | AST body analysis |
| `deferred_import_cycle` | Function-level imports to avoid circular deps | AST: Import nodes inside FunctionDef |
| `global_mutable_config` | Mutable module-level state mutated externally | AST + cross-module mutation detection |
| `inconsistent_return_type` | Functions returning None, dict, or str on different paths | AST return statement analysis |
| `inline_class_definition` | Classes defined inside functions | AST: ClassDef parent check |
| `hardcoded_url` | URLs as inline string literals | Regex in string literals |
| `printf_style_format` | Mixed `%s` and f-strings | Count formatting styles per file |
| `emoji_in_logs` | Emojis in log messages | Regex for Unicode emoji ranges |

The issue is at https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify/issues/3. Want me to continue with the remaining fix tasks (cancellation check dedup, dead code removal, etc.)?

## Summary of Changes

### Exception Narrowing (Files 1-5)

**1. `/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/params/phase.py`** (line 81)
- `except Exception` changed to `except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError)`
- Context: Parsing phase config parameters via `parse_phase_config()`. Failures are config/parameter parsing errors (bad values, missing keys, wrong types).

**2. `/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/params/phase_config.py`** (lines 59, 82)
- Two `except Exception` blocks changed to `except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError)`
- Context: `apply_phase_config_patch()` patches an ML model definition in memory by importing `wgp`, reading model definitions, and deep-copying configs. `restore_model_patches()` restores the original model definition. These are ML model operations that can fail with runtime errors, value errors, or OS-level errors when loading libraries.

**3. `/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/params/phase_multiplier_utils.py`** (line 260)
- `except Exception` changed to `except (ValueError, TypeError)`
- Context: `extract_phase_values()` calls `parse_phase_multiplier()` which parses semicolon-separated float strings. The only expected failures are `ValueError` (invalid float) or `TypeError` (wrong input type), matching the exceptions already caught inside `parse_phase_multiplier()` itself.

**4. `/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/platform_utils.py`** (line 50)
- `except Exception` changed to `except ImportError`
- Context: The function tries to `import ctypes` and use it to load `libasound.so.2`. The prior except clauses already handle `OSError` (library not found) and `AttributeError` (ctypes API issues). The remaining catch-all only needs to cover `ImportError` if ctypes itself cannot be imported.

**5. `/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/model_handlers/qwen_handler.py`** (lines 124, 148, 432)
- Three `except Exception` blocks changed to `except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError)`:
  - Line 124: `create_qwen_masked_composite()` -- downloads images via HTTP, opens with PIL, resizes, composites, and saves. Failures are image/video processing errors (network `OSError`, bad image `ValueError`, PIL `RuntimeError`).
  - Line 148: `_download_lora_if_missing()` -- downloads a LoRA file from HuggingFace Hub. Failures are file I/O and network errors (`OSError`), validation errors (`ValueError`), or hub errors (`RuntimeError`).
  - Line 432: Reference image download in `handle_qwen_image_style()` -- calls `download_image_if_url()` which does HTTP fetch + file write. Same failure modes as the composite creation.

### Magic Number Extraction (Files 6-8)

**6. `/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/structure_video_guidance.py`** (line 1316)
- Changed `len(response.content) / 1024 / 1024` to `len(response.content) / BYTES_PER_MB`

     ```python
     def _extract_video_thumbnail(video_path: Path, task_id_str: str) -> Path | None:
         """Extract a thumbnail from a video file.
         Checks for an existing poster (.jpg alongside the video) first.
         Falls back to extracting the first frame via OpenCV.
         Returns:
             Path to the thumbnail file (caller must clean up temp files), or None.
     ```

     ```python
     def _check_orchestrator_cancelled(orchestrator_task_id: str, context_msg: str, dprint=print) -> str | None:
         """Check if orchestrator was cancelled and cancel children if so.
         Returns an error message string if cancelled, None if still active.
         """
         status = db_ops.get_task_current_status(orchestrator_task_id)
         if status and status.lower() in ('cancelled', 'canceled'):
             dprint(f"[CANCELLATION] Join orchestrator {orchestrator_task_id} was cancelled - {context_msg}")
             db_ops.cancel_orchestrator_children(orchestrator_task_id, reason="Orchestrator cancelled by user")
             return f"Orchestrator cancelled: {context_msg}"
         return None
     ```

## Overall File Structure Assessment

### Current Layout (post-decomposition)

```
root/
├── worker.py                  # Entry point (463 LOC) - OK
├── headless_wgp.py           # WanOrchestrator (1649 LOC) - facade + class
├── headless_model_management.py  # HeadlessTaskQueue (728 LOC) - facade + class
├── heartbeat_guardian.py      # Entry point (267 LOC) - OK
├── create_test_task.py        # Utility script (647 LOC)
├── debug.py                   # CLI entry point (189 LOC) - OK
│
├── source/
│   ├── common_utils.py        # Re-export facade (16 LOC) ✓
│   ├── video_utils.py         # Re-export facade (8 LOC) ✓
│   ├── db_operations.py       # Re-export facade + ConfigProxy (72 LOC) ✓
│   ├── constants.py           # Shared constants ✓
│   │
│   ├── visualization_utils.py  # 1094 LOC - NOT decomposed
│   ├── vlm_utils.py            # 933 LOC - NOT decomposed
│   ├── logging_utils.py        # 904 LOC - NOT decomposed
│   ├── structure_video_guidance.py  # 1905 LOC - NOT decomposed
│   ├── travel_segment_processor.py  # 671 LOC - NOT decomposed
│   ├── specialized_handlers.py # 195 LOC
│   ├── platform_utils.py       # 76 LOC
│   ├── uni3c_validation.py     # 331 LOC
│   │
│   ├── utils/          # Decomposed from common_utils ✓
│   ├── video/          # Decomposed from video_utils ✓
│   ├── db/             # Decomposed from db_operations ✓
│   ├── wgp/            # Decomposed from headless_wgp ✓
│   ├── task_queue/     # Decomposed from headless_model_management ✓
│   ├── comfy/          # ComfyUI handler (2 files)
│   ├── lora/           # LoRA management (2 files)
│   ├── model_handlers/ # Qwen handler (1 file)
│   ├── params/         # Parameter dataclasses (8 files) ✓
│   ├── tasks/          # Task registry + conversion (3 files)
│   ├── worker/         # Worker utilities (3 files)
│   │
│   ├── task_handlers/
│   │   ├── join_clips.py          # Re-export facade (9 LOC) ✓
│   │   ├── travel_between_images.py  # Re-export facade (13 LOC) ✓
│   │   ├── join_clips_orchestrator.py  # 1385 LOC - NOT decomposed
│   │   ├── edit_video_orchestrator.py  # 661 LOC
│   │   ├── magic_edit.py              # 197 LOC
│   │   ├── inpaint_frames.py          # 310 LOC
│   │   ├── create_visualization.py    # 134 LOC
│   │   ├── join/       # Decomposed from join_clips ✓
│   │   └── travel/     # Decomposed from travel_between_images ✓
│   │
│   └── Wan2GP/         # Vendored (symlink?)
│
├── debug/              # Debug CLI tool
├── tests/
├── examples/
├── scripts/

└── Wan2GP/             # Vendored
```

### Structural Issues (most to least significant)

**1. The "half-decomposed" problem** — This is the biggest thing. You decomposed 5 major files into subpackages (`utils/`, `video/`, `db/`, `wgp/`, `task_queue/`, `join/`, `travel/`) but left 5 comparable files un-decomposed as loose files in `source/`:

| File | LOC | Has subpackage peer? |
|------|-----|---------------------|
| `structure_video_guidance.py` | 1905 | No |
| `visualization_utils.py` | 1094 | No |
| `vlm_utils.py` | 933 | No |
| `logging_utils.py` | 904 | No |
| `travel_segment_processor.py` | 671 | Related to `travel/` |

This creates an inconsistent pattern: some domains live in packages, some are loose files. A newcomer would wonder "why does `video_utils.py` get a package but `visualization_utils.py` at 1094 LOC doesn't?"

The most actionable fix: `travel_segment_processor.py` clearly belongs inside `source/task_handlers/travel/`. The rest are judgment calls — at ~1000 LOC they're not urgently in need of decomposition, but the inconsistency is noticeable.

**2. `source/` is a grab-bag namespace** — The `source/` directory mixes:
- Re-export facades (`common_utils.py`, `video_utils.py`, `db_operations.py`)
- Actual implementation files (`visualization_utils.py`, `vlm_utils.py`, etc.)
- Sub-packages (`utils/`, `video/`, `db/`, etc.)
- Constants (`constants.py`)

There's no clear rule for "what lives at `source/` top level vs. in a subpackage." The facades exist for backward compat, which is fine, but they sit next to real 1000+ LOC files which muddies the distinction.

**3. Root-level files** — `headless_wgp.py` (1649 LOC) and `headless_model_management.py` (728 LOC) are still at the project root alongside entry points. They're facades now but they're *big* facades — `headless_wgp.py` still has the full `WanOrchestrator` class definition (the methods just delegate to `source/wgp/`). This is unusual — typically the class would live in the package and the root file would just re-export it.

**4. Naming inconsistency in task_handlers/** — You have:
- `join_clips.py` → facade for `join/`
- `travel_between_images.py` → facade for `travel/`
- `join_clips_orchestrator.py` → **1385 LOC actual implementation** (not decomposed, no subpackage)
- `edit_video_orchestrator.py` → 661 LOC actual implementation

The pattern "some `_foo.py` files are facades, some are implementations" is confusing. `join_clips_orchestrator.py` at 1385 LOC is comparable in size to the files that were decomposed.

**5. Singular-file subpackages** — Several packages contain just 1-2 files:
- `source/model_handlers/` — just `qwen_handler.py`
- `source/lora/` — just `lora_paths.py` + `lora_utils.py`
- `source/comfy/` — just `comfy_handler.py` + `comfy_utils.py`

These are fine if they're expected to grow, but if `model_handlers/` will only ever have the Qwen handler, the package overhead doesn't add much.

**6. `debug/` vs `debug.py`** — Having both a `debug/` directory (the debug CLI tool's package) and a `debug.py` file (entry point) at root level is a Python antipattern. If someone does `import debug`, which one do they get? It works because `debug.py` presumably isn't imported as a module, but it's still confusing.

### What's Actually Good

- **The decomposition pattern is solid** — facades preserving backward compat while real code lives in focused submodules. This is the right approach.
- **`source/params/`** — Well-organized, 8 focused files, clear domain.
- **`source/db/`** — Clean split: config, edge_helpers, task_claim, task_completion, task_polling, task_dependencies. Each file has a clear responsibility.
- **`source/video/`** — Good domain-driven split (crossfade, ffmpeg_ops, frame_extraction, etc.)
- **`source/utils/`** — Sensible decomposition of the utility grab-bag into domain-specific utils.
- **Entry points are clear** — `worker.py`, `debug.py`, `heartbeat_guardian.py` are obviously entry points.

### If I Were Suggesting a "Clean" Target Structure

The main moves would be:
1. Move `travel_segment_processor.py` into `source/task_handlers/travel/`
2. Either decompose or accept `structure_video_guidance.py`, `visualization_utils.py`, `vlm_utils.py`, `logging_utils.py` as-is (they're not *that* big)
3. Move the `WanOrchestrator` class body into `source/wgp/orchestrator.py`, make `headless_wgp.py` a thin re-export
4. Same for `HeadlessTaskQueue` → `source/task_queue/`

But honestly, most of these are cosmetic. The decomposition work already done is the high-value structural improvement. The remaining loose files are in the 600-1900 LOC range which is large but not pathological, and disrupting them further has diminishing returns.
